<div class="col-sm order-1">
      <h2 class="chapter_title">CHAPTER 317A. NONPROFIT CORPORATIONS</h2>
      
      <h1 class="fgh">GENERAL PROVISIONS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.001">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.001 CITATION.</h1>
        <p>This chapter may be cited as the "Minnesota Nonprofit Corporation Act."</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.001.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.1.0">1989 c 304 s 1</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.011">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.011 DEFINITIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Scope.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this chapter, the terms in this section have the meanings given them, unless the language or context clearly shows that a different meaning is intended.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Address.</span></h2>
          <p>"Address" means mailing address, including a zip code, except that in the case of a registered office, address means the mailing address and the actual office location, which may not be a post office box.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Articles.</span></h2>
          <p>"Articles" means, in the case of a corporation incorporated under or governed by this chapter, articles of incorporation, articles of amendment, a resolution of election to become governed by this chapter, a statement of change of registered office, registered agent, or name of registered agent, articles of merger, articles of consolidation, articles of abandonment, and articles of dissolution. In the case of a foreign corporation, the term includes documents serving a similar function required to be filed with the secretary of state or other officer of the corporation's state of incorporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.3a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3a.<span class="headnote">Authenticated.</span></h2>
          <p>"Authenticated" means, with respect to an electronic communication, that the communication is delivered to the principal place of business of the corporation, or to an officer or agent of the corporation authorized by the corporation to receive the communication, and that the communication sets forth information from which the corporation can reasonably conclude that the communication was sent by the purported sender.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.3b">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3b.<span class="headnote">Ballot.</span></h2>
          <p>"Ballot" means a written ballot or a ballot transmitted by electronic communication.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Board of directors.</span></h2>
          <p>"Board of directors" or "board" means the group of persons vested with the general management of the internal affairs of a corporation, regardless of how they are identified.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>"Bylaws" means the code adopted for the regulation or management of the internal affairs of a corporation, regardless of how designated.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Corporation" means a corporation that is governed by this chapter. A corporation may not:</p>
          <p>(1) be formed for a purpose involving pecuniary gain to its members, other than to members that are nonprofit organizations or subdivisions, units, or agencies of the United States or a state or local government; and</p>
          <p>(2) pay dividends or other pecuniary remuneration, directly or indirectly, to its members, other than to members that are nonprofit organizations or subdivisions, units, or agencies of the United States or a state or local government.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Director.</span></h2>
          <p>"Director" means a member of the board.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.7a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7a.<span class="headnote">Electronic communication.</span></h2>
          <p>"Electronic communication" means any form of communication, not directly involving the physical transmission of paper, that creates a record that may be retained, retrieved, and reviewed by a recipient of the communication, and that may be directly reproduced in paper form by the recipient through an automated process.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Filed with the secretary of state.</span></h2>
          <p>"Filed with the secretary of state" means that a document meeting the requirements of this chapter, signed, and accompanied by a filing fee of $35, has been delivered to the secretary of state of this state. The secretary of state shall endorse on the original the word "Filed" and the month, day, and year of filing, record the document in the Office of the Secretary of State, and return a document to the person who delivered it for filing.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Foreign corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>"Foreign corporation" means a corporation that is formed under laws other than the laws of this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Good faith.</span></h2>
          <p>"Good faith" means honesty in fact in the conduct of an act or transaction.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Legal representative.</span></h2>
          <p>"Legal representative" means a person empowered to act for another person, including, but not limited to, an agent, officer, partner, or associate of an organization; a trustee of a trust; a personal representative; a trustee in bankruptcy; or a receiver, guardian, custodian, or conservator.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Member.</span></h2>
          <p>"Member" means a person with membership rights in a corporation under its articles or bylaws, regardless of how the person is identified.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Members with voting rights.</span></h2>
          <p>"Members with voting rights" means members or a class of members that has voting rights with respect to the purpose or matter involved.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Notice.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Notice" is given by a member of a corporation to the corporation or an officer of the corporation when in writing and mailed or delivered to the corporation or the officer at the registered office of the corporation.</p>
          <p>(b) Notice is given by the corporation to a director, officer, member, or other person:</p>
          <p>(1) when mailed to the person at an address designated by the person, at the last known address of the person or, in the case of a director, officer, or member, at the address of the person in the corporate records;</p>
          <p>(2) when communicated to the person orally;</p>
          <p>(3) when handed to the person;</p>
          <p>(4) when left at the office of the person with a clerk or other person in charge of the office, or if there is no one in charge, when left in a conspicuous place in the office;</p>
          <p>(5) if the person's office is closed or the person to be notified has no office, when left at the dwelling or usual place of abode of the person with a person of suitable age and discretion residing in the house;</p>
          <p>(6) when provided to the person by means of electronic communication as provided under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.231">317A.231</a> or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.450">317A.450</a>; or</p>
          <p>(7) when the method is fair and reasonable when all the circumstances are considered.</p>
          <p>(c) Notice by mail is given when deposited in the United States mail with sufficient postage. Notice is considered received when it is given.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Officer.</span></h2>
          <p>"Officer" means the president, the treasurer, however designated, a person elected, appointed, or otherwise designated as an officer pursuant to section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.311">317A.311</a>, and a person deemed elected an officer under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.321">317A.321</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Organization" means a domestic or foreign business or nonprofit corporation, limited liability company, whether domestic or foreign, partnership, limited partnership, joint venture, association, trust, estate, enterprise, or other legal or commercial entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Registered office.</span></h2>
          <p>"Registered office" means the place in this state designated in the articles of a corporation as the registered office of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Related organization.</span></h2>
          <p>"Related organization" means an organization that controls, is controlled by, or is under common control with, another corporation. Control exists if an organization:</p>
          <p>(1) owns, directly or indirectly, at least 50 percent of the stock ownership or membership interests of another organization;</p>
          <p>(2) has the right, directly or indirectly, to elect, appoint, or remove 50 percent or more of the members with voting rights of the governing body of another organization; or</p>
          <p>(3) has the power, directly or indirectly, to direct or cause the direction of the management and policies of another organization, whether through the ownership of voting interests, by contract, or otherwise.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.18a">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18a.<span class="headnote">Remote communication.</span></h2>
          <p>"Remote communication" means communication via electronic communication, conference telephone, video conference, the Internet, or such other means by which persons not physically present in the same location may communicate with each other on a substantially simultaneous basis.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Signed.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) "Signed" means that the signature of a person is written on a document, as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/645.44#stat.645.44.14">645.44, subdivision 14</a>. A document required by this chapter to be filed with the secretary of state must be signed by a person authorized to do so by this chapter, the articles or bylaws, or a resolution approved by the directors, as required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.237">317A.237</a>, or the members with voting rights, if any, if required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.443">317A.443</a>.</p>
          <p>(b) A signature on a document may be a facsimile affixed, engraved, printed, placed, stamped with indelible ink, transmitted by facsimile or electronically, or in any other manner reproduced on the document.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.011.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Written action.</span></h2>
          <p>"Written action" means a record signed by all persons required to take the action or consented to by authenticated electronic communication by all persons required to take the action. The term also means the counterparts of a record signed or consented to by authenticated electronic communication by any of the persons taking the action. A counterpart is the action of the persons signing or consenting to it, and all counterparts are one written action by all persons signing or consenting to them.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.011.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.2.0">1989 c 304 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.203.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 203</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/488/#laws.0.1.0">1990 c 488 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/503/#laws.0.2.0">1992 c 503 s 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/517/#laws.1.19.0">1992 c 517 art 1 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/10/#laws.4.3.0">1997 c 10 art 4 s 3</a>,4; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/311/#laws.3.1.0">2002 c 311 art 3 s 1</a>-3; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/199/#laws.14.30.0">2004 c 199 art 14 s 30</a>,31; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.1.11.0">2010 c 250 art 1 s 11</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/17/#laws.0.1.0">2017 c 17 s 1</a>,2,19</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.015">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.015 LEGAL RECOGNITION OF ELECTRONIC RECORDS AND SIGNATURES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.015.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Definitions.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) For purposes of this section, the words, terms, and phrases defined in this subdivision have the meanings given them.</p>
          <p>(b) "Electronic" means relating to technology having electrical, digital, magnetic, wireless, optical, electromagnetic, or similar capabilities.</p>
          <p>(c) "Electronic record" means a record created, generated, sent, communicated, received, or stored by electronic means.</p>
          <p>(d) "Electronic signature" means an electronic sound, symbol, or process attached to or logically associated with a record and executed or adopted by a person with the intent to sign the record.</p>
          <p>(e) "Record" means information that is inscribed on a tangible medium or that is stored in an electronic or other medium and is retrievable in perceivable form.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.015.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Electronic records and signatures.</span></h2>
          <p>For purposes of this chapter:</p>
          <p>(1) a record or signature may not be denied legal effect or enforceability solely because it is in electronic form;</p>
          <p>(2) a contract may not be denied legal effect or enforceability solely because an electronic record was used in its formation;</p>
          <p>(3) if a provision requires a record to be in writing, an electronic record satisfies the requirement; and</p>
          <p>(4) if a provision requires a signature, an electronic signature satisfies the requirement.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.015.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/311/#laws.3.4.0">2002 c 311 art 3 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">APPLICATION</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.021">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.021 APPLICATION AND ELECTION.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Election by chapter 300, 309, or 315 corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation incorporated under or governed by chapter 300, 309, or 315 that has not later become governed by Minnesota Statutes 1988, chapter 317, may elect to be governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Election by corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>On or after August 1, 1989, and before January 1, 1991, a corporation incorporated under or governed by Minnesota Statutes 1988, chapter 317, may elect to become governed by this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Conforming articles of electing corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>If the articles of an electing corporation include a provision prohibited by this chapter, omit a provision required by this chapter, or are inconsistent with this chapter, the electing corporation shall amend its articles to conform to the requirements of this chapter. The appropriate provisions of the corporation's articles or bylaws or the law by which it was governed before the effective date of the election made under this section control the manner of adoption of the amendment.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Method of election.</span></h2>
          <p>An election by a corporation to become governed by this chapter must be made by resolution approved by the affirmative vote of the members with voting rights of the same proportion that is required for amendment of the articles of the corporation before the election. If there are no members with voting rights, the corporation must elect to be governed by this chapter by a resolution adopted by a majority vote of the directors entitled to vote at a meeting of the board, with proper notice given. The notice must include a statement that a purpose of the meeting is to consider an election to become governed by this chapter. The resolution, articles of amendment, if required, and a certified copy of corporate documents previously recorded with the county recorder that would be filed with the secretary of state under this chapter, must be filed with the secretary of state and are effective upon filing. If an amendment of the articles is not required, the resolution must state that the articles of the corporation conform to the requirements of this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Effect of election upon bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon filing an election under subdivision 4, provisions of the bylaws that are consistent with this chapter remain or become effective and provisions of the bylaws that are inconsistent with this chapter are not effective.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Choice of incorporation until January 1, 1990.</span></h2>
          <p>From August 1, 1989, to December 31, 1990, a corporation may be incorporated under this chapter or under Minnesota Statutes 1988, chapter 317.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Nonelecting nonprofit corporations subject to this chapter as of January 1, 1991.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A corporation in existence on January 1, 1991, that is within the scope of this chapter and incorporated under another statute of this state, other than a corporation incorporated under chapter 300, 309, or 315 that has not later become governed by Minnesota Statutes 1988, chapter 317, is governed by this chapter as of January 1, 1991, as though the corporation had been incorporated under this chapter. The provisions of the articles and bylaws of the corporation that may be included in the articles or bylaws under this chapter remain in effect. The provisions of the articles and bylaws of the corporation that are inconsistent with this chapter are not effective as of January 1, 1991. Provisions required by this chapter to be contained in the articles that do not appear in the articles are read into them as a matter of law.</p>
          <p>(b) On and after January 1, 1991, a corporation that elected to reject Laws 1951, chapter 550, sections 1 to 25, that does not elect to be governed by this entire chapter is governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.131">317A.131</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.151">317A.151</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.461">317A.461</a>; and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.601">317A.601</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.791">317A.791</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Incorporation after January 1, 1991.</span></h2>
          <p>On and after January 1, 1991, a corporation that is within the scope of this chapter may be incorporated only under this chapter.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.021.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Applicability of other laws.</span></h2>
          <p>Chapters 316, 317, and 556 do not apply to corporations.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.021.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.3.0">1989 c 304 s 3</a>,137; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/488/#laws.0.2.0">1990 c 488 s 2</a>-5; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1991/0/199/#laws.1.70.0">1991 c 199 art 1 s 70</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2002/0/400/#laws.0.1.0">2002 c 400 s 1</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/4/#laws.0.55.0">2005 c 4 s 55</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/69/#laws.3.22.0">2005 c 69 art 3 s 22</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.022">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.022 ELECTION BY CERTAIN CHAPTER 318 ASSOCIATIONS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.022.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2011/0/106/#laws.0.27.0">2011 c 106 s 27</a>]</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.022.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Amended title and other conforming amendments.</span></h2>
          <p>The declaration of trust, as defined in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/318.02#stat.318.02.1">318.02, subdivision 1</a>, of the association must be amended to identify it as the "articles of an association electing to be treated as a nonprofit corporation." All references in this chapter to "articles" or "articles of incorporation" include the declaration of trust of an electing association. If the declaration of trust includes a provision prohibited by this chapter for inclusion in articles of incorporation, omits a provision required by this chapter to be included in articles of incorporation, or is inconsistent with this chapter, the electing association shall amend its declaration of trust to conform to the requirements of this chapter. The appropriate provisions of the association's declaration of trust or bylaws or chapter 318 control the manner of adoption of the amendments required by this subdivision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.022.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Method of election.</span></h2>
          <p>An election by an association under subdivision 2 must be made by resolution approved by the affirmative vote of the trustees of the association and by the affirmative vote of the members or other persons with voting rights in the association. The affirmative vote of both the trustees of the association and of the members or other persons with voting rights, if any, in the association must be of the same proportion that is required for an amendment of the declaration of trust of the association before the election, in each case upon proper notice that a purpose of the meeting is to consider an election by the association to cease to be an association subject to and governed by chapter 318 and to become and be a nonprofit corporation subject to and governed by this chapter. The resolution and the articles of the amendment of the declaration of trust must be filed with the secretary of state and are effective upon filing, or a later date as may be set forth in the filed resolution. Upon the effective date, without any other action or filing by or on behalf of the association, the association automatically is subject to this chapter in the same manner and to the same extent as though it had been formed as a nonprofit corporation pursuant to this chapter. Upon the effective date of the election, the association is not considered to be a new entity, but is considered to be a continuation of the same entity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.022.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Effects of election.</span></h2>
          <p>Upon the effective date of an association's election under subdivision 3, and consistent with the continuation of the association under this chapter:</p>
          <p>(1) the organization has the rights, privileges, immunities, powers, and is subject to the duties and liabilities, of a corporation formed under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(2) all real or personal property, debts, including debts arising from a subscription for membership and interests belonging to the association, continue to be the real and personal property, and debts of the organization without further action;</p>
          <p>(3) an interest in real estate possessed by the association does not revert to the grantor, or otherwise, nor is it in any way impaired by reason of the election, and the personal property of the association does not revert by reason of the election;</p>
          <p>(4) except where the will or other instrument provides otherwise, a devise, bequest, gift, or grant contained in a will or other instrument, in a trust or otherwise, made before or after the election has become effective, to or for the association, inures to the organization;</p>
          <p>(5) the debts, liabilities, and obligations of the association continue to be the debts, liabilities, and obligations of the organization, just as if the debts, liabilities, and obligations had been incurred or contracted by the organization after the election;</p>
          <p>(6) existing claims or a pending action or proceeding by or against the association may be prosecuted to judgment as though the election had not been affected;</p>
          <p>(7) the liabilities of the trustees, members, officers, directors, or similar groups or persons, however denominated, of the association, are not affected by the election;</p>
          <p>(8) the rights of creditors or liens upon the property of the association are not impaired by the election;</p>
          <p>(9) an electing association may merge with one or more nonprofit corporations in accordance with the applicable provisions of this chapter, and either the association or a nonprofit corporation may be the surviving entity in the merger; and</p>
          <p>(10) the provisions of the bylaws of the association that are consistent with this chapter remain or become effective and provisions of the bylaws that are inconsistent with this chapter are not effective.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.022.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1994/0/625/#laws.8.66.0">1994 c 625 art 8 s 66</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.031">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.031 TRANSITION; CONTINUATION OF LEGAL ACTS.</h1>
        <p>The continuation or completion of an act by a corporation that is not incorporated under, but has become governed by, this chapter, and the continuation or performance of an executed or wholly or partially executory contract, conveyance, or transfer to or by the corporation, is valid if otherwise lawful before the corporation became governed by this chapter. The act may be continued, completed, enforced, or ended as required or permitted by a statute applicable before the date on which the corporation became governed by this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.031.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.4.0">1989 c 304 s 4</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.041">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.041 RESERVATION OF RIGHT.</h1>
        <p>The state reserves the right to amend or repeal this chapter. A corporation governed by this chapter is subject to this reserved right.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.041.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.5.0">1989 c 304 s 5</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.051">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.051 SCOPE OF CHAPTER.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.051.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General.</span></h2>
          <p>This chapter does not apply to cooperative associations, public cemetery corporations and associations, and private cemeteries.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.051.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Religious corporations.</span></h2>
          <p>This chapter does not apply to a religious corporation authorized by chapter 315 unless it is formed under this chapter or elects to be governed by this chapter as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.021">317A.021</a>. Regardless of whether it is formed or elects to be governed by this chapter, a religious corporation may elect to be governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.601">317A.601</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.671">317A.671</a> without electing to come under the entire chapter. If a religious corporation elects to be governed by sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.601">317A.601</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.671">317A.671</a>, it shall record its documents with the county recorder of the county where its registered office is located instead of the secretary of state.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.051.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.6.0">1989 c 304 s 6</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2005/0/4/#laws.0.56.0">2005 c 4 s 56</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.061">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.061 FOREIGN NONPROFIT CORPORATIONS; SECTIONS APPLICABLE.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.061.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">General.</span></h2>
          <p>Except for this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.651">317A.651</a> concerning merger or consolidation, this chapter does not apply to foreign nonprofit corporations.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.061.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Sections applicable.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) Except as provided in paragraph (b), a foreign nonprofit corporation is subject to chapter 303. Unless it complies with chapter 303, a foreign corporation may not transact business in this state.</p>
          <p>(b) Sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/303.02#stat.303.02.2">303.02, subdivision 2</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/303.07">303.07</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/303.14">303.14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/303.16">303.16</a>, subdivision 2, clause (6); and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/303.22">303.22</a> do not apply to foreign nonprofit corporations.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.061.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.7.0">1989 c 304 s 7</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/40/#laws.1.109.0">2017 c 40 art 1 s 109</a></p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">INCORPORATION; ARTICLES</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.101">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.101 PURPOSES.</h1>
        <p>A corporation may be incorporated under this chapter for any lawful purpose, unless another statute requires incorporation for a purpose under a different law. Unless otherwise limited in its articles, a corporation has a general purpose of engaging in any lawful activity. A corporation engaging in conduct that is regulated by another statute is subject to the limitations of the other statute.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.101.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.8.0">1989 c 304 s 8</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.105">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.105 INCORPORATORS.</h1>
        <p>One or more adult natural persons may act as incorporators of a corporation by filing articles of incorporation for the corporation with the secretary of state.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.105.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.9.0">1989 c 304 s 9</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.111">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.111 ARTICLES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Required provisions.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles of incorporation must contain:</p>
          <p>(1) the name of the corporation;</p>
          <p>(2) the address of the registered office of the corporation and the name of its registered agent, if any, at that address; and</p>
          <p>(3) the name and address of each incorporator.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Statutory provisions that may be modified only in articles.</span></h2>
          <p>The following provisions govern a corporation unless modified in the articles:</p>
          <p>(1) a corporation has a general purpose of engaging in any lawful activity (section 317A.101);</p>
          <p>(2) the power to initially adopt, amend, or repeal the bylaws is vested in the board (section 317A.181);</p>
          <p>(3) cumulative voting for directors is prohibited (section 317A.215);</p>
          <p>(4) a written action by the board taken without a meeting must be signed by all directors (section 317A.239); and</p>
          <p>(5) members are of one class (section 317A.401).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Statutory provisions that may be modified in articles or bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>The following provisions govern a corporation unless modified in the articles or bylaws:</p>
          <p>(1) a certain method must be used for amending the articles (section 317A.133);</p>
          <p>(2) a corporation has perpetual duration and certain powers (section 317A.161);</p>
          <p>(3) certain procedures apply to the adoption, amendment, or repeal of bylaws by the members with voting rights (section 317A.181);</p>
          <p>(4) a director holds office until expiration of the director's term and election of a successor (section 317A.207);</p>
          <p>(5) the term of a director filling a vacancy expires at the end of the term the director is filling (section 317A.207);</p>
          <p>(6) the compensation of directors is fixed by the board (section 317A.211);</p>
          <p>(7) a certain method must be used for removal of directors (section 317A.223);</p>
          <p>(8) a certain method must be used for filling board vacancies (section 317A.227);</p>
          <p>(9) board meetings must be held at least once per year, and if the board fails to select a place for a board meeting, it must be held at the registered office (section 317A.231);</p>
          <p>(10) a director may call a board meeting, and the notice of the meeting need not state the purpose of the meeting (section 317A.231);</p>
          <p>(11) a majority of the board is a quorum (section 317A.235);</p>
          <p>(12) the affirmative vote of the majority of directors present is required for board action (section 317A.237);</p>
          <p>(13) a committee consists of one or more persons, who need not be directors, appointed by the board (section 317A.241, subdivision 2), and a committee may create one or more subcommittees and may delegate to a subcommittee any, or all, of the authority of the committee (section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.241#stat.317A.241.3">317A.241, subdivision 3</a>);</p>
          <p>(14) the president and treasurer have certain duties, until the board determines otherwise (section 317A.305);</p>
          <p>(15) a certain method must be used for removal of officers (section 317A.341);</p>
          <p>(16) officers may delegate some or all of their duties and powers, if not prohibited by the board from doing so (section 317A.351);</p>
          <p>(17) a corporation does not have members (section 317A.401);</p>
          <p>(18) the board may determine the consideration required to admit members (section 317A.401);</p>
          <p>(19) all members are entitled to vote and have equal rights and preferences in matters (section 317A.401);</p>
          <p>(20) memberships may not be transferred (section 317A.405);</p>
          <p>(21) a corporation with members with voting rights must hold a regular meeting of members with voting rights annually (section 317A.431);</p>
          <p>(22) if a specific minimum notice period has not been fixed by law, at least five days' notice is required for a meeting of members (section 317A.435);</p>
          <p>(23) the board may fix a date up to 60 days before the date of a members meeting as the date for determination of the members entitled to notice of and entitled to vote at the meeting (section 317A.437);</p>
          <p>(24) each member with voting rights has one vote (section 317A.441);</p>
          <p>(25) the affirmative vote of the majority of members with voting rights present and entitled to vote is required for action of the members, unless this chapter or the articles or bylaws require a greater vote or voting by class (section 317A.443);</p>
          <p>(26) members with voting rights may take action at a meeting by voice or ballot, by unanimous action without a meeting, by mailed ballot, or by electronic communication (section 317A.443);</p>
          <p>(27) the number of members required for a quorum is ten percent of the members entitled to vote (section 317A.451);</p>
          <p>(28) certain procedures govern acceptance of member acts (section 317A.455); and</p>
          <p>(29) indemnification of certain persons is required (section 317A.521).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Optional provisions; specific subjects.</span></h2>
          <p>The following provisions relating to the management or regulation of the affairs of a corporation may be included in the articles or, except for naming members of the first board, in the bylaws:</p>
          <p>(1) the first board of directors may be named in the articles (section 317A.171);</p>
          <p>(2) additional qualifications for directors may be imposed (section 317A.205);</p>
          <p>(3) terms of directors may be staggered (section 317A.207);</p>
          <p>(4) the day or date, time, and place of board meetings may be fixed (section 317A.231);</p>
          <p>(5) in addition to the president, authority to sign and deliver certain documents may be delegated to an officer or agent of the corporation (section 317A.305);</p>
          <p>(6) additional officers may be designated (section 317A.311);</p>
          <p>(7) additional powers, rights, duties, and responsibilities may be given to officers (section 317A.311);</p>
          <p>(8) a method for filling vacant offices may be specified (section 317A.341);</p>
          <p>(9) membership criteria and procedures for admission may be established (section 317A.401);</p>
          <p>(10) membership terms may be fixed (section 317A.401);</p>
          <p>(11) a corporation may levy dues, assessments, or fees on members (section 317A.407);</p>
          <p>(12) a corporation may buy memberships (section 317A.413);</p>
          <p>(13) a corporation may have delegates with some or all the authority of members (section 317A.415);</p>
          <p>(14) the day or date, time, and place of regular member meetings or the place of special meetings may be fixed (section 317A.431);</p>
          <p>(15) certain persons may be authorized to call special meetings of members (section 317A.433);</p>
          <p>(16) notices of special member meetings may be required to contain certain information (section 317A.433);</p>
          <p>(17) a larger than majority vote may be required for member action (section 317A.443);</p>
          <p>(18) members with voting rights may vote by proxy (section 317A.453); and</p>
          <p>(19) members with voting rights may enter into voting agreements (section 317A.457).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Optional provisions; generally.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles may contain other provisions consistent with law relating to the management or regulation of the affairs of the corporation.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Powers need not be stated.</span></h2>
          <p>It is not necessary to state the corporate powers granted by this chapter in the articles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Substantive law controls.</span></h2>
          <p>If there is a conflict between subdivision 2, 3, or 4 and another section of this chapter, the other section controls.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.111.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Dependence on facts outside of the articles.</span></h2>
          <p>Except for provisions included pursuant to subdivision 1, a provision of the articles may:</p>
          <p>(1) be made dependent upon facts ascertainable outside the articles, but only if the manner in which the facts operate upon the provision is clearly and expressly set forth in the articles; and</p>
          <p>(2) incorporate by reference some or all of the terms of an agreement, contract, or other arrangement entered into by the corporation, but only if the corporation retains at its principal executive office a copy of the agreement, contract, or other arrangement or the portions incorporated by reference.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.111.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.10.0">1989 c 304 s 10</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/488/#laws.0.6.0">1990 c 488 s 6</a>,7; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/503/#laws.0.3.0">1992 c 503 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.1.12.0">2010 c 250 art 1 s 12</a>-14; art 2 s 16; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/17/#laws.0.19.0">2017 c 17 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.113">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.113 PRIVATE FOUNDATIONS; PROVISIONS CONSIDERED CONTAINED IN ARTICLES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.113.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Provisions required.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles of incorporation of a corporation that is a private foundation as defined in section 509(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986 and an instrument governing the use, retention, or disposition by the corporation of its income or property must contain the provisions contained in this section. If the articles and instrument do not contain these provisions, they are considered to have incorporated the language in clauses (1) to (5) with the same effect as though the language was set forth verbatim. Except as provided in subdivision 2, these provisions govern the corporation as to the use, retention, and disposition of its income and property regardless of provisions of the articles or instrument or other law of this state to the contrary:</p>
          <p>(1) the corporation shall distribute for each of its taxable years amounts at least sufficient to avoid liability for the tax imposed by section 4942(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986;</p>
          <p>(2) the corporation may not engage in an act of "self-dealing" as defined in section 4941(d) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986 that would give rise to liability for the tax imposed by section 4941(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986;</p>
          <p>(3) the corporation may not retain "excess business holdings" as defined in section 4943(c) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986 that would give rise to liability for the tax imposed by section 4943(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986;</p>
          <p>(4) the corporation may not make investments that would jeopardize the carrying out of the exempt purposes of the corporation, within the meaning of section 4944 of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986, so as to give rise to liability for the tax imposed by section 4944(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986; and</p>
          <p>(5) the corporation may not make a "taxable expenditure" as defined in section 4945(d) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986 that would give rise to liability for the tax imposed by section 4945(a) of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.113.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Exception.</span></h2>
          <p>Subdivision 1 does not apply to a corporation if a court of competent jurisdiction determines that the application would be contrary to the terms of an instrument described in subdivision 1 and that the instrument may not properly be changed to conform to subdivision 1.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.113.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Future references.</span></h2>
          <p>A reference in subdivision 1 to a particular section of the Internal Revenue Code of 1986 includes the corresponding provision of a future United States Internal Revenue law.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.113.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Application.</span></h2>
          <p>This section applies to all corporations that could be governed by this chapter, notwithstanding sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.021">317A.021</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.051">317A.051</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.113.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Rights reserved.</span></h2>
          <p>This section does not impair the rights and powers of the attorney general or the courts of this state with respect to a corporation.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.113.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.11.0">1989 c 304 s 11</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.115">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.115 CORPORATE NAME.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Requirements.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The corporate name must be in the English language or in another language expressed in English letters or characters.</p>
          <p>(b) A corporate name may not contain a word or phrase that shows or implies that it may not be incorporated under this chapter.</p>
          <p>(c) A corporate name need not contain the word "corporation," "incorporated," "company," or "limited," or an abbreviation of one of these words.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Name must be distinguishable.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A corporate name must be distinguishable upon the records in the Office of the Secretary of State from the name of a domestic corporation or limited partnership, a foreign corporation or limited partnership, whether profit or nonprofit, a limited liability company, whether domestic or foreign, on file, authorized to do business in this state at the time of filing, a limited liability partnership, whether domestic or foreign, or a name the right to which is, at the time of incorporation, reserved, registered, or provided for in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.35">5.35</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.117">317A.117</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.117">302A.117</a>, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/321.0109">321.0109</a>, or <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/322C.0109">322C.0109</a>, or sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>, unless one of the following is filed with the articles:</p>
          <p>(1) the written consent of the organization having the name that is not distinguishable;</p>
          <p>(2) a certified copy of a final decree of a court in this state establishing the prior right of the applicant to use its corporate name in this state; or</p>
          <p>(3) an affidavit of nonuse of the kind required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/302A.115#stat.302A.115.1">302A.115, subdivision 1</a>, paragraph (d), clause (3).</p>
          <p>(b) The secretary of state shall determine whether a name is distinguishable from another name for purposes of this section and section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.117">317A.117</a>.</p>
          <p>(c) This subdivision does not affect the right of a corporation existing on January 1, 1991, or a foreign corporation authorized to do business in this state on that date, to use its corporate name.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Other laws affecting use of names.</span></h2>
          <p>This section and sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.117">317A.117</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.823#stat.317A.823.2">317A.823, subdivision 2</a>, do not abrogate or limit the law of unfair competition or unfair practices, sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.001">333.001</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/333.54">333.54</a>, the laws of the United States with respect to the right to acquire and protect copyrights, trade names, trademarks, service names, service marks, or other rights to the exclusive use of names or symbols, nor derogate the common law or the principles of equity.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Use of name by successor corporation.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation that is merged with another domestic or foreign corporation, that is incorporated by the reorganization of one or more domestic or foreign corporations, or that acquires by sale, lease, or other disposition to or exchange with a domestic corporation all or substantially all of the assets of another domestic or foreign corporation, including its name, may have the same name as that used in this state by any of the other corporations, if the other corporation was incorporated under the laws of, or is authorized to transact business in, this state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Effect of wrongful use; injunction.</span></h2>
          <p>The use of a name by a corporation in violation of this section does not affect or impair its corporate existence, but a court in this state may, upon application of the state or of an interested or affected person, enjoin the corporation from doing business under a name assumed in violation of this section, although its articles may have been filed with the secretary of state and a certificate of incorporation issued.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.115.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.</h2>
          <p>[Repealed, <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2008/0/203/#laws.0.14.0">2008 c 203 s 14</a>]</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.115.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/292/#laws.0.12.0">1989 c 292 s 12</a>,13; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.12.0">1989 c 304 s 12</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/488/#laws.0.8.0">1990 c 488 s 8</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1992/0/517/#laws.1.20.0">1992 c 517 art 1 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1995/0/128/#laws.2.3.0">1995 c 128 art 2 s 3</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2004/0/199/#laws.13.112.0">2004 c 199 art 13 s 112</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2009/0/98/#laws.0.15.0">2009 c 98 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2014/0/157/#laws.2.24.0">2014 c 157 art 2 s 24</a>,29,31</p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.117">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.117 RESERVED NAME.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.117.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Who may reserve.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporate name permitted by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.115">317A.115</a> may be reserved in the records of the secretary of state by:</p>
          <p>(1) a person doing business in this state under that name;</p>
          <p>(2) a person intending to incorporate under this chapter;</p>
          <p>(3) a domestic corporation intending to change its name;</p>
          <p>(4) a foreign corporation intending to make application for a certificate of authority to transact business in this state;</p>
          <p>(5) a foreign corporation authorized to transact business in this state and intending to change its name;</p>
          <p>(6) a person intending to incorporate a foreign corporation and intending to have the foreign corporation make application for a certificate of authority to transact business in this state; or</p>
          <p>(7) a foreign corporation doing business under that name or a name deceptively similar to that name in a state other than this state and not described in clauses (4) to (6).</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.117.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Method of reservation.</span></h2>
          <p>The reservation must be made by filing with the secretary of state a request that the name be reserved. If the name is available for reservation by the applicant, the secretary of state shall reserve the name for the applicant for 12 months. The reservation may be renewed for successive 12-month periods.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.117.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Transfer of reservation.</span></h2>
          <p>The right to a corporate name reserved under this section may be transferred to another person by or on behalf of the applicant for whom the name was reserved by filing with the secretary of state a notice of the transfer and specifying the name and address of the transferee.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.117.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/292/#laws.0.14.0">1989 c 292 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.13.0">1989 c 304 s 13</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.317A.121"><b>317A.121</b> MS 2008 [Subds 1 and 2 repealed by amendment, 2010 c 250 art 2 s 17]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.121">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.121 REGISTERED OFFICE; REGISTERED AGENT.</h1>
        <p>Every corporation shall have a registered office, and may have a registered agent, in the manner prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.36">5.36</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.121.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.14.0">1989 c 304 s 14</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.2.17.0">2010 c 250 art 2 s 17</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="sr" id="stat.317A.123"><b>317A.123</b> MS 2008 [Subds 1 to 3 repealed by amendment, 2010 c 250 art 2 s 18]</div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.123">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.123 CHANGE OF REGISTERED OFFICE OR REGISTERED AGENT; CHANGE OF NAME OF REGISTERED AGENT.</h1>
        <p>Every corporation may change its registered office or change its registered agent, and the agent may resign or change its business address or name, in the manner prescribed by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/5.36">5.36</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.123.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.15.0">1989 c 304 s 15</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2001/1/10/#laws.2.73.0">1Sp2001 c 10 art 2 s 73</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.2.18.0">2010 c 250 art 2 s 18</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.131">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.131 AMENDMENT OF ARTICLES.</h1>
        <p>The articles of a corporation may be amended to include or modify a provision that is required or permitted to appear in the articles or to omit a provision not required to be included in the articles. When articles are amended to restate them, the name and address of each incorporator may be omitted. Unless otherwise provided in this chapter, the articles may be amended or modified only under sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.133">317A.133</a> and <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.139">317A.139</a>.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.131.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.16.0">1989 c 304 s 16</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.133">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.133 PROCEDURE FOR AMENDMENT OF ARTICLES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.133.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Approval by incorporators or board.</span></h2>
          <p>A majority of incorporators may amend the articles by written action if no directors are named in the original articles, if no directors have been elected, and if there are no members with voting rights. A majority of all directors may amend the articles if there are no members with voting rights, if members with voting rights have authorized the board to amend the articles under subdivision 3, or if the amendment merely restates the existing articles, as amended. Notice of the meeting and of the proposed amendment must be given to the board. An amendment restating the existing articles may, but need not, be submitted to and approved by the members with voting rights as provided in subdivision 2.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.133.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Approval by board and members with voting rights.</span></h2>
          <p>Amendments to the articles must be approved by the affirmative vote of a majority of all directors and by the members with voting rights. If an amendment is initiated by the directors, proper notice of the proposed amendment must precede a meeting of the members with voting rights at which the amendment will be considered and must include the substance of the proposed amendment. If an amendment is proposed and approved by the members with voting rights, those members may demand a special board meeting within 60 days for consideration of the proposed amendment if a regular board meeting would not occur within 60 days.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.133.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Approval by board where members have voting rights.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) The members with voting rights may authorize the board of directors, subject to paragraph (c), to exercise from time to time the power of amendment of the articles without approval of the members with voting rights.</p>
          <p>(b) When the members with voting rights have authorized the board of directors to amend the articles, the board of directors, by the affirmative vote of a majority of all directors, unless the articles, bylaws, or the members' resolution authorizing the board action require a greater vote, may amend the articles at a meeting of the board. Notice of the meeting and of the proposed amendment must be given to the board.</p>
          <p>(c) The members with voting rights may prospectively revoke the authority of the board to exercise the power of the members to amend the articles at a meeting duly called for that purpose.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.133.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Restriction of approval methods.</span></h2>
          <p>Articles or bylaws may require greater than majority approval by the board or approval by greater than a majority of a quorum of the members with voting rights for an action under this section and may limit or prohibit the use of mail ballots by members with voting rights.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.133.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Approval of class.</span></h2>
          <p>The articles or bylaws may provide that an amendment also must be approved by the members of a class.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.133.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.17.0">1989 c 304 s 17</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1990/0/488/#laws.0.9.0">1990 c 488 s 9</a>-12; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2010/0/250/#laws.1.15.0">2010 c 250 art 1 s 15</a>-17; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/2017/0/17/#laws.0.19.0">2017 c 17 s 19</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.139">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.139 ARTICLES OF AMENDMENT.</h1>
        <p>When an amendment has been adopted, articles of amendment must be prepared that contain:</p>
        <p>(1) the name of the corporation;</p>
        <p>(2) the amendment adopted;</p>
        <p>(3) with respect to an amendment restating the articles, a statement that the amendment correctly sets forth without change the corresponding provisions of the articles as previously amended, if the amendment was approved only by the board; and</p>
        <p>(4) a statement that the amendment has been adopted under this chapter.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.139.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.18.0">1989 c 304 s 18</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.141">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.141 EFFECT OF AMENDMENT.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.141.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Effect on cause of action.</span></h2>
          <p>An amendment does not affect an existing cause of action in favor of or against the corporation, a pending suit to which the corporation is a party, nor the existing rights of persons other than members.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.141.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Effect of change of name.</span></h2>
          <p>If the corporate name is changed by the amendment, a suit brought by or against the corporation under its former name does not abate for that reason.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.141.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Effect of amendments restating articles.</span></h2>
          <p>When effective under section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.151">317A.151</a>, an amendment restating the articles in their entirety supersedes the original articles and amendments to the original articles.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.141.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Effect of amendments on charitable trust assets.</span></h2>
          <p>Assets held by a corporation, including income or fees from services, are restricted to the uses and purposes for which the property was received and held.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.141.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.19.0">1989 c 304 s 19</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1997/0/222/#laws.0.42.0">1997 c 222 s 42</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.151">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.151 FILING; EFFECTIVE DATE OF ARTICLES.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.151.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Filing required.</span></h2>
          <p>Articles of incorporation and articles of amendment must be filed with the secretary of state.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.151.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Effective date.</span></h2>
          <p>Articles of incorporation are effective and corporate existence begins when the articles of incorporation are filed with the secretary of state accompanied by a payment of $70, which includes a $35 incorporation fee in addition to the $35 filing fee required by section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.011#stat.317A.011.8">317A.011, subdivision 8</a>. Articles of amendment are effective when filed with the secretary of state or at another time within 31 days after filing if the articles of amendment so provide.</p>
        </div>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.151.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first"><a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.20.0">1989 c 304 s 20</a>; <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/335/#laws.1.204.0">1989 c 335 art 1 s 204</a></p>
      </div>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.155">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.155 PRESUMPTION; CERTIFICATE OF INCORPORATION.</h1>
        <p>When the articles of incorporation have been filed with the secretary of state and the required fee has been paid to the secretary of state, it is presumed that conditions precedent required to be performed by the incorporators have been complied with and that the corporation has been incorporated, and the secretary of state shall issue a certificate of incorporation to the corporation. This presumption does not apply against this state in a proceeding to cancel or revoke the certificate of incorporation or to compel the involuntary dissolution of the corporation.</p>
      </div>
      <div class="history" id="stat.317A.155.history
			">
        <h2>History: </h2>
        <p class="first">
          <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/laws/1989/0/304/#laws.0.21.0">1989 c 304 s 21</a>
        </p>
      </div>
      <h1 class="fgh">POWERS</h1>
      <div class="section" id="stat.317A.161">
        <h1 class="shn">317A.161 POWERS.</h1>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.1">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subdivision 1.<span class="headnote">Generally; limitations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation has the powers in this section, subject to limitations provided in applicable federal or state law or in its articles or bylaws.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.2">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 2.<span class="headnote">Duration.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation has perpetual duration.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.3">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 3.<span class="headnote">Legal capacity.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may sue and be sued, and participate in a legal, administrative, or arbitration proceeding, in its corporate name.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.4">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 4.<span class="headnote">Property ownership.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may buy, lease, acquire, own, hold, improve, use, and deal in and with, real or personal property, or an interest in property, wherever located.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.5">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 5.<span class="headnote">Property disposition.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may sell, convey, mortgage, create a security interest in, lease, exchange, transfer, or dispose of all or a part of its real or personal property, or an interest in property, wherever located.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.6">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 6.<span class="headnote">Trading in securities; obligations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may buy, subscribe for, acquire, own, hold, vote, use, employ, sell, exchange, mortgage, lend, create a security interest in, dispose of, use, and deal in and with, securities or other interests in, or obligations of, a person or direct or indirect obligations of a domestic or foreign government or instrumentality.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.7">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 7.<span class="headnote">Contracts; mortgages.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make contracts and incur liabilities, borrow money, issue its securities, and secure its obligations by mortgage of or creation of a security interest in its property, franchises, and income.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.8">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 8.<span class="headnote">Investment.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may invest and reinvest its funds.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.9">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 9.<span class="headnote">Holding property as security.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may take and hold real and personal property, whether or not of a kind sold or otherwise dealt in by the corporation, as security for the payment of money loaned, advanced, or invested.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.10">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 10.<span class="headnote">Location.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may conduct its business, carry on its operations, have offices, and exercise the powers granted by this chapter anywhere in the universe.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.11">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 11.<span class="headnote">Donations.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make donations for religious, scientific, educational, or charitable purposes, and for other purposes consistent with law, that further the corporate interest.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.12">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 12.<span class="headnote">Pensions; benefits.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may pay pensions, retirement allowances, and compensation for past services and establish employee or incentive benefit plans, trusts, and provisions for the benefit of its officers, directors, employees, and agents and their families, dependents, and beneficiaries. It may indemnify and buy insurance for a fiduciary of an employee benefit and incentive plan, trust, or provision.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.13">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 13.<span class="headnote">Participations.</span></h2>
          <p>(a) A corporation may participate in the promotion, organization, management, and operation of an organization or in a transaction, undertaking, or arrangement that the participating corporation would have power to conduct by itself, whether or not the participation involves sharing or delegation of control.</p>
          <p>(b) A corporation may participate with others in a corporation, partnership, limited partnership, joint venture, trust, or other association of any kind that the participating corporation would have power to conduct by itself, whether or not the participation involves sharing or delegation of control.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.14">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 14.<span class="headnote">Insurance.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may provide for its benefit life insurance and other insurance with respect to the services of its officers, directors, employees, and agents.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.15">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 15.<span class="headnote">Corporate seal.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may have, alter at pleasure, and use a corporate seal as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.163">317A.163</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.16">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 16.<span class="headnote">Bylaws.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may adopt, amend, and repeal bylaws relating to the management of the business or the regulation of the affairs of the corporation as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.181">317A.181</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.17">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 17.<span class="headnote">Committees.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may establish committees of the board of directors, elect or appoint persons to the committees, define their duties, and fix their compensation as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.241">317A.241</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.18">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 18.<span class="headnote">Officers; employees; agents.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may elect or appoint officers, employees, and agents of the corporation, define their duties, and fix their compensation as provided in sections <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.301">317A.301</a> to <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.361">317A.361</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.19">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 19.<span class="headnote">Loans; guaranties; sureties.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may lend money to, guarantee an obligation of, become a surety for, or otherwise financially assist persons as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.501">317A.501</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.20">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 20.<span class="headnote">Advances.</span></h2>
          <p>A corporation may make advances to its directors, officers, and employees and those of its subsidiaries as provided in section <a href="https://www.revisor.mn.gov/statutes/cite/317A.505">317A.505</a>.</p>
        </div>
        <div class="subd" id="stat.317A.161.21">
          
          <h2 class="subd_no">Subd. 21.<span class="headnote">Indemnification.</span></h2>
